R/W Read/write register field.
R/W1C You can read this register field. Writing 1 clears it.
RC/W1C Reading this register field clears it. Writing 1 clears it.
R/Wo You can read this register field. You can only write to it once.
Attribute Description
Volatile As defined by the IP-XACT specification. If true, indicates in the case of a write 
followed by read, or in the case of two consecutive reads, there is no guarantee as to 
what is returned by the read on the second transaction or that this return value is 
consistent with the write or read of the first transaction. The element implies there is 
some additional mechanism by which this field can acquire new values other than by 
reads/writes/resets and other access methods known to IP-XACT. For example, 
when the IP updates the register field contents.

Testable As defined by the IP-XACT specificatio n. Possible values are unconstrained, 
untestable, readOnly, writeAsRead, restore. Untestable means that this field is 
untestable by a simple automated register test. For example, the read-write access 
of the register is controlled by a pin or another register. readOnly means that you 
should not write to this register; only read from it. This might apply for a register that 
modifies the contents of another register.
Reset Mask As defined by the IP-XACT specification. Indicates that this register field has an 
unknown reset value. For example, the reset value is set by another register or an 
input pin; or the register is implemented using RAM.
* Varies Indicates that the memory access (or reset) attribute (read, write behavior) is not 
fixed. For example, the read-write access of the register is controlled by a pin or 
another register. Or when the access depends on some configuration parameter; in 
this case the post-configuration report in coreConsultant gives the actual access 
value.

11.1.1 ANA_OVRDVAL_0 
■ Name: (RXS) Internal Override Value Register 0 
■ Description: Register bits act as override value at the mux which can override values on (RXS) 
analog-digital interface ports. Note that when the respective override enable bit is 0b0, the readback 
of this register returns the original value of the signal. Register 0.

■ Offset: 0x0 + j*0x100 + 0x000

11.2.22 ANA_O VRDVAL_13 
■ Name: SUP ANA Output Register 13 
■ Description: Register bits act as override value at the mux which can override values on (SUP) 
analog-digital interface ports. Register 13. When the respective override enable bit is 0b0, the readback 
of this register returns the original value of the signal. 
■ Size: 32 bits 
■ Offset: 0x1054

15 rt_manual_txup_go R/W Enables TxUP tune to continue in manual tune mode, when 
TYPE is TxUP tune. When in non TxUP manual tune mode, 
this bit must be 0. When in TxUP manual tune mode and after 
TxUP manual tune is triggered, if the read only register SUP. 
RTUNE_STAT.DTB_RTUNE equals to 2'b01, it is required to 
first release the rtune related asynchronous override on 
analog/digital interface then turn this bit TXUP_GO from low to 
high to continue TxUP manual tune which also indicates that 
this bit needs to be low when triggering TxUP manual tune.
Value After Reset: 0x0
14:5 rt_set_val R/W Value to use when triggering SET_VAL field:  
Values: 
■ 0x0 (ADC): ADC, or read/write rt_value.
■ 0x1 (RX_TUNE): Rx tune, or read/write rx_cal_val (only 6 
bits).
■ 0x2 (TX_DN): TxDN tune, or read/write txdn_cal_val (10 
bits).
■ 0x3 (TX_UP): TxUP tune, or read/write txup_cal_val (10 
bits) or Resref detect (no affect when triggering SET_VAL 
field).
Value After Reset: 0x0
4:3 rt_acc_type R/W Type of manual tuning or register read/write to execute.
Value After Reset: 0x0
2 rt_set_val_en R/W This bit sets value. Write 1 to manually write the register 
specified by the TYPE field to the value in the VALUE field.
Value After Reset: 0x0
1 rt_manual_tune R/W Write 1 to perform manual tuning specified by TYPE field. 
Starting a manual tune while a tune is currently running can 
cause unpredictable results. For use only when you know what 
the part is doing (w.r.t. resistor tuning).
Value After Reset: 0x0
0 rt_flip_comp_result R/W Invert analog comparator output.
Value After Reset: 0x0

11:9 dbg_clkout_sel R/W Select signal to bring-out different internal clocks for 
observation on (PIN::(TXS)::tx_dbg_clk_o). Available options: 
Values: 
■ 0x0 (ZERO): 0
■ 0x1 (SYMB_INT_8): SYMCLK_INT/8
■ 0x2 (SYMB_INT_16): SYMCLK_INT/16
■ 0x3 (DLL_4): DLL_CLK/4
■ 0x4 (DLL_8): DLL_CLK/8
■ 0x5 (CAL_COMP): CAL_COMP_CLK
■ 0x6 (SYMB_8): SYMCLK/8
■ 0x7 (SYMB_16): SYMCLK/16
Value After Reset: 0x0
8:5 pattern_sel R/W Pattern selection. These bits also determine the kind of 
incoming data pattern for the pattern matcher. Note that the 
pattern matcher does not support the JP03B and Transmitter 
linearity patterns. This field is programmed as follows. Values 
not listed below are reserved. 
Values: 
■ 0x0 (PRBS7): PRBS7
■ 0x4 (PRBS15): PRBS15
■ 0x6 (PRBS31): PRBS31
■ 0x8 (PRQS10): PRQS10
■ 0xb (CUSTM): Custom pattern defined by 
(APB::(TXS)::TST_CFG::custom_pattern)
Value After Reset: 0x0
4:2 pos_err R/W Controls the position, in the word to serialize, of the error 
inserted by gen_error: 
Values: 
■ 0x0 (ERRF): 4 bits of error inserted in the 4 LSBs
■ 0x1 (ERR1): 1 bit of error inserted in the LSB
■ 0x2 (ERR2): 1 bit of error inserted in the 2nd LSB
■ 0x3 (ERR3): 1 bit of error inserted in the 3rd LSB
■ 0x4 (ERR4): 1 bit of error inserted in the 4th LSB
Value After Reset: 0x0
1 gen_err R/W A write to 0b1 inserts 1 bit error into the ongoing pattern 
generation. Write 0b0 to 0b1 to create new errors.
Value After Reset: 0x0
0 patgen_en R/W Write to 0b1 to enable the pattern generator. (TXS) ignores 
input parallel data at its input.
Value After Reset: 0x0

15 rt_manual_txup_go R/W Enables TxUP tune to continue in manual tune mode, when 
TYPE is TxUP tune. When in non TxUP manual tune mode, 
this bit must be 0. When in TxUP manual tune mode and after 
TxUP manual tune is triggered, if the read only register SUP. 
RTUNE_STAT.DTB_RTUNE equals to 2'b01, it is required to 
first release the rtune related asynchronous override on 
analog/digital interface then turn this bit TXUP_GO from low to 
high to continue TxUP manual tune which also indicates that 
this bit needs to be low when triggering TxUP manual tune.
Value After Reset: 0x0
14:5 rt_set_val R/W Value to use when triggering SET_VAL field:

■ 0x0 (ADC): ADC, or read/write rt_value.
■ 0x1 (RX_TUNE): Rx tune, or read/write rx_cal_val (only 6 
bits).
■ 0x2 (TX_DN): TxDN tune, or read/write txdn_cal_val (10 
bits).
■ 0x3 (TX_UP): TxUP tune, or read/write txup_cal_val (10 
bits) or Resref detect (no affect when triggering SET_VAL 
field).
Value After Reset: 0x0
4:3 rt_acc_type R/W Type of manual tuning or register read/write to execute.
Value After Reset: 0x0

specified by the TYPE field to the value in the VALUE field.
Value After Reset: 0x0
1 rt_manual_tune R/W Write 1 to perform manual tuning specified by TYPE field. 
Starting a manual tune while a tune is currently running can 
cause unpredictable results. For use only when you know what 
the part is doing (w.r.t. resistor tuning).
Value After Reset: 0x0

Value After Reset: 0x0
